    68→    _layout_ = "ms"  # suppress Python 3.13 deprecation — layout is identical on Linux
    69→    _fields_ = [
    70→        ("ptr_R",           ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 0   — splitData (logits) [splits,q,heads,v_dim] f32
    71→        ("_p0",             _p2),               # offset 8
    72→        ("ptr_LSE",         ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 16  — splitLse (attn_lse) [splits,q,heads] f32
    73→        ("_p1",             _p2),               # offset 24
    74→        ("ptr_Q",           ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 32  — Q [total_q, heads, qk_head_dim] fp8
    75→        ("_p2",             _p2),               # offset 40
    76→        ("ptr_KV",          ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 48  — KV [pages, ps, kv_heads, qk_head_dim] fp8
    77→        ("_p3",             _p2),               # offset 56
    78→        ("ptr_LTP",         ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 64  — kv_indptr [batch+1] int32
    79→        ("_p4",             _p2),               # offset 72
    80→        ("ptr_LTD",         ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 80  — kv_page_indices [total_kv_pages] int32
    81→        ("_p5",             _p2),               # offset 88
    82→        ("ptr_LTL",         ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 96  — kv_last_page_lens [batch] int32
    83→        ("_p6",             _p2),               # offset 104
    84→        ("scalar",          ctypes.c_float),   # offset 112 — softmax_scale
    85→        ("_p12",            _p3),               # offset 116
    86→        ("s_MQA",           ctypes.c_uint32),  # offset 128 — gqa_ratio * max_seqlen_q
    87→        ("_p13",            _p3),               # offset 132
    88→        ("s_kv_split",      ctypes.c_uint32),  # offset 144 — number of KV splits
    89→        ("_p14",            _p3),               # offset 148
    90→        ("s_Q_Bs",          ctypes.c_uint32),  # offset 160 — Q stride bytes per seq
    91→        ("_p15",            _p3),               # offset 164
    92→        ("s_Bs",            ctypes.c_uint32),  # offset 176 — KV page stride bytes
    93→        ("_p16",            _p3),               # offset 180
    94→        ("s_log2_plen",     ctypes.c_uint32),  # offset 192 — log2(page_size)
    95→        ("_p17",            _p3),               # offset 196
    96→        ("ptr_QTP",         ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 208 — qo_indptr [batch+1] int32
    97→        ("_p18",            _p2),               # offset 216
    98→        ("ptr_STP",         ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 224 — persistent metadata (work_meta_data ptr)
    99→        ("_p19",            _p2),               # offset 232
   100→        ("ptr_RP",          ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 240 — final output [total_q, heads, v_dim] bf16
   101→        ("_p20",            _p2),               # offset 248
   102→        ("ptr_QSCALE",      ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 256 — q_scale [1] f32
   103→        ("_p21",            _p2),               # offset 264
   104→        ("ptr_KVSCALE",     ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 272 — kv_scale [1] f32
   105→        ("_p22",            _p2),               # offset 280
   106→        ("out_16_nosplit",  ctypes.c_uint32),  # offset 288 — = kv_split (duplicate)
   107→        ("_p23",            _p3),               # offset 292
   108→        ("ptr_LSEP",        ctypes.c_void_p),  # offset 304 — final LSE (nullable)
   109→        ("_p24",            _p2),               # offset 312
   110→        #                                       # total: 320 bytes
   111→    ]
   112→
   113→
   114→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   115→# Module-level HIP setup — runs once at import time, not in the hot path
   116→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   117→
   118→# Verify struct layout before doing anything else
   119→assert ctypes.sizeof(MlaKernelArgs) == 320, (
   120→    f"MlaKernelArgs size mismatch: got {ctypes.sizeof(MlaKernelArgs)}, expected 320"
   121→)
   122→
   123→# Load HIP runtime
   124→_hip = ctypes.CDLL("libamdhip64.so")
   125→
   126→# Declare restype/argtypes to prevent ctypes from guessing (and segfaulting)
   127→_hip.hipModuleLoad.restype = ctypes.c_int
   128→_hip.hipModuleLoad.argtypes = [ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p), ctypes.c_char_p]
   129→
   130→_hip.hipModuleGetFunction.restype = ctypes.c_int
   131→_hip.hipModuleGetFunction.argtypes = [
   132→    ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p),
   133→    ctypes.c_void_p,
   134→    ctypes.c_char_p,
   135→]
   136→
   137→_hip.hipModuleLaunchKernel.restype = ctypes.c_int
   138→_hip.hipModuleLaunchKernel.argtypes = [
   139→    ctypes.c_void_p,                                        # function handle
   140→    ctypes.c_uint32, ctypes.c_uint32, ctypes.c_uint32,     # grid x/y/z
   141→    ctypes.c_uint32, ctypes.c_uint32, ctypes.c_uint32,     # block x/y/z
   142→    ctypes.c_uint32,                                        # shared memory bytes
   143→    ctypes.c_void_p,                                        # stream
   144→    ctypes.c_void_p,                                        # kernelParams (must be nullptr)
   145→    ctypes.c_void_p,                                        # extra (config array)
   146→]
   147→
   148→# Load the .co module and retrieve the function handle
   149→_mla_module = ctypes.c_void_p(0)
   150→_mla_func   = ctypes.c_void_p(0)
   151→
   152→_mla_err_load = _hip.hipModuleLoad(ctypes.byref(_mla_module), _CO_PATH)
   153→assert _mla_err_load == 0, f"hipModuleLoad failed with error code {_mla_err_load}"
   154→
   155→_mla_err_func = _hip.hipModuleGetFunction(
   156→    ctypes.byref(_mla_func), _mla_module, _KERNEL_SYMBOL
   157→)
   158→assert _mla_err_func == 0, f"hipModuleGetFunction failed with error code {_mla_err_func}"
   159→
   160→# ── Metadata + intermediate cache ──
   161→_cache: dict = {}
   162→
   163→
   164→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   165→# Helper functions
   166→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   167→
   168→def _choose_num_kv_splits(total_kv: int) -> int:
   169→    if total_kv <= 2048:
   170→        return 1
   171→    if total_kv <= 16384:
   172→        return 4
   173→    if total_kv <= 131072:
   174→        return 8
   175→    if total_kv <= 524288:
   176→        return 16
   177→    return 32
   178→
   179→
   180→def _quantize_fp8(tensor: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   181→    finfo = torch.finfo(FP8_DTYPE)
   182→    amax = tensor.abs().amax().clamp(min=1e-12)
   183→    scale = amax / finfo.max
   184→    fp8 = (tensor / scale).clamp(min=finfo.min, max=finfo.max).to(FP8_DTYPE)
   185→    return fp8, scale.to(torch.float32).reshape(1)
   186→
   187→
   188→def _get_cached_metadata(
   189→    bs: int, qseqlen: int, kvseqlen: int,
   190→    q_dtype: torch.dtype, kv_dtype: torch.dtype,
   191→    qo_indptr: torch.Tensor, kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   192→    num_kv_splits: int,
   193→):
   194→    key = (bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen, q_dtype, kv_dtype, num_kv_splits)
   195→    if key in _cache:
   196→        return _cache[key]
   197→
   198→    nq, nkv = NUM_HEADS, NUM_KV_HEADS
   199→    kv_last_page_len = (kv_indptr[1:] - kv_indptr[:-1]).to(torch.int32)
   200→
   201→    info = get_mla_metadata_info_v1(
   202→        bs, qseqlen, nq, q_dtype, kv_dtype,
   203→        is_sparse=False, fast_mode=False,
   204→        num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits, intra_batch_mode=True,
   205→    )
   206→    work = [torch.empty(s, dtype=t, device="cuda") for s, t in info]
   207→    work_metadata, work_indptr, work_info_set, reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map = work
   208→
   209→    get_mla_metadata_v1(
   210→        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_last_page_len,
   211→        nq // nkv, nkv, True,
   212→        work_metadata, work_info_set, work_indptr,
   213→        reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map,
   214→        page_size=PAGE_SIZE, kv_granularity=max(PAGE_SIZE, 16),
   215→        max_seqlen_qo=qseqlen, uni_seqlen_qo=qseqlen,
   216→        fast_mode=False, max_split_per_batch=num_kv_splits,
   217→        intra_batch_mode=True, dtype_q=q_dtype, dtype_kv=kv_dtype,
   218→    )
   219→
   220→    total_kv_len = int(kv_indptr[-1].item())
   221→    kv_indices = torch.arange(total_kv_len, dtype=torch.int32, device="cuda")
   222→
   223→    # Pre-allocate intermediates
   224→    total_q = bs * qseqlen
   225→    logits   = torch.empty((num_kv_splits, total_q, nq, V_HEAD_DIM), dtype=torch.float32, device="cuda")
   226→    attn_lse = torch.empty((num_kv_splits, total_q, nq), dtype=torch.float32, device="cuda")
   227→    output   = torch.empty((total_q, nq, V_HEAD_DIM), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device="cuda")
   228→
   229→    meta = {
   230→        "work_meta_data":    work_metadata,
   231→        "work_indptr":       work_indptr,
   232→        "work_info_set":     work_info_set,
   233→        "reduce_indptr":     reduce_indptr,
   234→        "reduce_final_map":  reduce_final_map,
   235→        "reduce_partial_map": reduce_partial_map,
   236→        "kv_indices":        kv_indices,
   237→        "kv_last_page_len":  kv_last_page_len,
   238→        "logits":            logits,
   239→        "attn_lse":          attn_lse,
   240→        "output":            output,
   241→    }
   242→    _cache[key] = meta
   243→    return meta
   244→
   245→
   246→def _launch_mla_stage1(
   247→    q_fp8: torch.Tensor,
   248→    kv_4d: torch.Tensor,
   249→    qo_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   250→    kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   251→    meta: dict,
   252→    q_scale: torch.Tensor,
   253→    kv_scale: torch.Tensor,
   254→    qseqlen: int,
   255→    num_kv_splits: int,
   256→) -> None:
   257→    """Launch mla_a8w8_qh16_qseqlen1_gqaratio16_ps directly via hipModuleLaunchKernel.
   258→
   259→    Replaces the Python wrapper `mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd`, eliminating the
   260→    per-call dispatch overhead while keeping the same kernel execution path.
   261→    """
   262→    logits   = meta["logits"]
   263→    attn_lse = meta["attn_lse"]
   264→    output   = meta["output"]
   265→
   266→    # ── Scalar computations (matching csrc/py_itfs_cu/asm_mla.cu) ──
   267→    # s_Q_Bs = Q.stride(0) * element_size * max_seqlen_q
   268→    s_Q_Bs = q_fp8.stride(0) * q_fp8.element_size() * qseqlen
   269→    # s_Bs   = KV.stride(0) * element_size
   270→    s_Bs   = kv_4d.stride(0) * kv_4d.element_size()
   271→    # s_log2_plen = log2(PAGE_SIZE) = log2(1) = 0
   272→    s_log2_plen = 0
   273→    # s_MQA  = gqa_ratio * max_seqlen_q = (NUM_HEADS / NUM_KV_HEADS) * qseqlen
   274→    s_MQA  = (NUM_HEADS // NUM_KV_HEADS) * qseqlen
   275→
   276→    # ── Build the packed KernelArgs struct ──
   277→    args = MlaKernelArgs()
   278→    args.ptr_R          = logits.data_ptr()
   279→    args.ptr_LSE        = attn_lse.data_ptr()
   280→    args.ptr_Q          = q_fp8.data_ptr()
   281→    args.ptr_KV         = kv_4d.data_ptr()
   282→    args.ptr_LTP        = kv_indptr.data_ptr()
   283→    args.ptr_LTD        = meta["kv_indices"].data_ptr()
   284→    args.ptr_LTL        = meta["kv_last_page_len"].data_ptr()
   285→    args.scalar         = SM_SCALE
   286→    args.s_MQA          = s_MQA
   287→    args.s_kv_split     = num_kv_splits
   288→    args.s_Q_Bs         = s_Q_Bs
   289→    args.s_Bs           = s_Bs
   290→    args.s_log2_plen    = s_log2_plen
   291→    args.ptr_QTP        = qo_indptr.data_ptr()
   292→    # work_meta_data IS the pre-packed 80-byte uint64 GPU buffer — pass ptr directly
   293→    args.ptr_STP        = meta["work_meta_data"].data_ptr()
   294→    args.ptr_RP         = output.data_ptr()
   295→    args.ptr_QSCALE     = q_scale.data_ptr()
   296→    args.ptr_KVSCALE    = kv_scale.data_ptr()
   297→    args.out_16_nosplit = num_kv_splits
   298→    args.ptr_LSEP       = None  # no final LSE output needed
   299→
   300→    # ── Build config array for BUFFER_POINTER launch style ──
   301→    arg_size = ctypes.c_size_t(ctypes.sizeof(args))
   302→    config = (ctypes.c_void_p * 5)(
   303→        HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_BUFFER_POINTER,
   304→        ctypes.cast(ctypes.addressof(args), ctypes.c_void_p),
   305→        HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_BUFFER_SIZE,
   306→        ctypes.cast(ctypes.addressof(arg_size), ctypes.c_void_p),
   307→        HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_END,
   308→    )
   309→
   310→    # ── Persistent mode grid: (work_indptr.size(0) - 1, 1, 1) ──
   311→    gdx = meta["work_indptr"].size(0) - 1
   312→    stream = torch.cuda.current_stream().cuda_stream
   313→
   314→    ret = _hip.hipModuleLaunchKernel(
   315→        _mla_func,
   316→        gdx, 1, 1,   # grid
   317→        256, 1, 1,   # block (4 wavefronts of 64 threads)
   318→        0,           # shared memory
   319→        stream,
   320→        None,        # kernelParams — must be nullptr for BUFFER_POINTER style
   321→        config,      # extra
   322→    )
   323→    if ret != 0:
   324→        raise RuntimeError(f"hipModuleLaunchKernel failed with error code {ret}")
   325→
   326→
   327→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   328→# Entry point
   329→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   330→
   331→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
   332→    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config = data
   333→
   334→    bs       = config["batch_size"]
   335→    qseqlen  = config["q_seq_len"]
   336→    kvseqlen = config["kv_seq_len"]
   337→    total_kv = bs * kvseqlen
   338→
   339→    # ── Regime 1: torch.matmul for small shapes ──
   340→    # Small batch or short context: avoid aiter overhead entirely
   341→    if bs <= MATMUL_MAX_BS or total_kv <= MATMUL_MAX_TOTAL_KV:
   342→        kv_bf16 = kv_data["bf16"]  # [total_kv, 1, 576]
   343→        q_3d    = q.view(bs, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)            # [bs, 16, 576]
   344→        kv_per  = kv_bf16.view(bs, kvseqlen, QK_HEAD_DIM)       # [bs, kvseqlen, 576]
   345→        scores  = torch.matmul(q_3d, kv_per.transpose(1, 2))    # [bs, 16, kvseqlen]
   346→        scores.mul_(SM_SCALE)
   347→        weights = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(torch.bfloat16)
   348→        v_per   = kv_per[:, :, :V_HEAD_DIM]                     # [bs, kvseqlen, 512]
   349→        out     = torch.matmul(weights, v_per)                   # [bs, 16, 512]
   350→        return out.view(bs * qseqlen, NUM_HEADS, V_HEAD_DIM)
   351→
   352→    # ── Regime 2+3: direct CK-Tile ASM dispatch ──
   353→    kv_buffer_fp8, kv_scale = kv_data["fp8"]
   354→    q_fp8, q_scale = _quantize_fp8(q)
   355→
   356→    num_kv_splits = _choose_num_kv_splits(total_kv)
   357→    kv_4d = kv_buffer_fp8.view(kv_buffer_fp8.shape[0], PAGE_SIZE, NUM_KV_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)
   358→
   359→    meta = _get_cached_metadata(
   360→        bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen,
   361→        q_fp8.dtype, kv_buffer_fp8.dtype,
   362→        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, num_kv_splits,
   363→    )
   364→
   365→    # Stage 1: direct hipModuleLaunchKernel (bypasses Python wrapper overhead)
   366→    _launch_mla_stage1(
   367→        q_fp8.view(-1, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM),
   368→        kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr,
   369→        meta, q_scale, kv_scale,
   370→        qseqlen, num_kv_splits,
   371→    )
   372→
   373→    # Stage 2: reduce (must remain via aiter — kernel lives in .so, not .co)
   374→    mla_reduce_v1(
   375→        meta["logits"], meta["attn_lse"],
   376→        meta["reduce_indptr"], meta["reduce_final_map"], meta["reduce_partial_map"],
   377→        qseqlen, meta["output"], None,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    return meta["output"]
   381→

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The runner path vs local path bug (`/home/mike-anderson/dev/aiter/` vs `/home/runner/aiter/`) is a classic dev/prod environment mismatch — particularly dangerous with ctypes because the `assert _mla_err_load == 0` at import time would produce a numeric HIP error code (likely `hipErrorFileNotFound = 301`) rather than a descriptive message. Always store runner paths as constants derived from the environment (e.g., `os.environ.get("AITER_PATH", "/home/runner/aiter")`) in production kernel code.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

     1→"""Popcorn CLI integration for submitting kernels to AMD MI355X leaderboards.
     2→
     3→Wraps popcorn-cli subprocess calls with structured output parsing.
     4→Handles test/benchmark/leaderboard modes, timeouts, and rate limiting.
     5→"""
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import logging
     9→import re
    10→import subprocess
    11→import time
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→POPCORN_CLI = Path.home() / ".local" / "bin" / "popcorn-cli"
    18→LUMA_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent  # luma_speedrun/
    19→
    20→# Kernel short name → (leaderboard name, submission directory)

"""MLA decode submission — three-regime routing with fast_mode=False.

Optimizations over reference (Phase 17 best):
1. Three-regime routing: matmul for small, aiter ASM for large
2. OR routing logic: bs<=4 OR total_kv<=32768 → matmul (captures both failure modes)
3. Adaptive num_kv_splits schedule (1/4/8/16/32 based on total_kv)
4. Direct ASM dispatch (bypasses mla_decode_fwd Python wrapper, saves ~3-5µs)
5. Metadata caching (8 tensors cached per shape key, 25% improvement)
6. fast_mode=False (CU work distribution is better on MI355X)
7. Pre-allocated intermediates (logits, attn_lse)

"""Popcorn CLI integration for submitting kernels to AMD MI355X leaderboards.

Wraps popcorn-cli subprocess calls with structured output parsing.
Handles test/benchmark/leaderboard modes, timeouts, and rate limiting.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import re
import subprocess
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

POPCORN_CLI = Path.home() / ".local" / "bin" / "popcorn-cli"
LUMA_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent  # luma_speedrun/

# Kernel short name → (leaderboard name, submission directory)
KERNEL_MAP: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {
    "gemm": ("amd-mxfp4-mm", "amd-mxfp4-mm"),
    "moe": ("amd-moe-mxfp4", "amd-moe-mxfp4"),
    "mla": ("amd-mixed-mla", "amd-mixed-mla"),
}

# Timeout per mode (seconds). JIT builds can take 230s+ for MoE.
MODE_TIMEOUTS: dict[str, int] = {
    "test": 720,
    "benchmark": 720,
    "leaderboard": 720,
}


@dataclass
class SubmitResult:
    """Structured result from a popcorn-cli submission."""
    passed: bool
    score: float  # geomean µs for benchmark, 0.0 for test-only
    mode: str
    kernel: str
    stderr: str  # full stderr (contains discovery probe output)
    stdout: str  # full stdout
    error: str  # error message if failed
    elapsed_s: float  # wall clock time

    @property
    def discovered_kernels(self) -> list[str]:
        """Extract FOUND lines from stderr (probe discovery output)."""
        return [line for line in self.stderr.splitlines() if line.startswith("FOUND ")]


def submit(
    kernel: str,
    submission_path: str | Path,
    mode: str = "test",
) -> SubmitResult:
    """Submit a kernel to popcorn-cli and parse the result.

    Args:
        kernel: Short name ("gemm", "moe", "mla") or full leaderboard name
        submission_path: Path to submission.py file
        mode: "test", "benchmark", or "leaderboard"

    Returns:
        SubmitResult with parsed output
    """
    # Resolve leaderboard name
    if kernel in KERNEL_MAP:
        leaderboard, _ = KERNEL_MAP[kernel]
    else:
        leaderboard = kernel  # assume it's already a full leaderboard name

    submission_path = Path(submission_path)
    if not submission_path.exists():
        return SubmitResult(
            passed=False, score=0.0, mode=mode, kernel=kernel,
            stderr="", stdout="", error=f"File not found: {submission_path}",
            elapsed_s=0.0,
        )
```

    52→class _p2(ctypes.Structure):

   113→
   114→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   115→# Module-level HIP setup — runs once at import time, not in the hot path
   116→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

   118→# Verify struct layout before doing anything else

   145→    ctypes.c_void_p,                                        # extra (config array)

   150→_mla_func   = ctypes.c_void_p(0)

   152→_mla_err_load = _hip.hipModuleLoad(ctypes.byref(_mla_module), _CO_PATH)

   154→
   155→_mla_err_func = _hip.hipModuleGetFunction(

   166→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   167→
   168→def _choose_num_kv_splits(total_kv: int) -> int:
   169→    if total_kv <= 2048:
   170→        return 1
   171→    if total_kv <= 16384:
   172→        return 4
   173→    if total_kv <= 131072:
   174→        return 8
   175→    if total_kv <= 524288:
   176→        return 16
   177→    return 32
   178→
   179→
   180→def _quantize_fp8(tensor: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   181→    finfo = torch.finfo(FP8_DTYPE)
   182→    amax = tensor.abs().amax().clamp(min=1e-12)
   183→    scale = amax / finfo.max
   184→    fp8 = (tensor / scale).clamp(min=finfo.min, max=finfo.max).to(FP8_DTYPE)
   185→    return fp8, scale.to(torch.float32).reshape(1)
   186→
   187→
   188→def _get_cached_metadata(
   189→    bs: int, qseqlen: int, kvseqlen: int,
   190→    q_dtype: torch.dtype, kv_dtype: torch.dtype,
   191→    qo_indptr: torch.Tensor, kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   192→    num_kv_splits: int,
   193→):
   194→    key = (bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen, q_dtype, kv_dtype, num_kv_splits)
   195→    if key in _cache:
   196→        return _cache[key]
   197→
   198→    nq, nkv = NUM_HEADS, NUM_KV_HEADS
   199→    kv_last_page_len = (kv_indptr[1:] - kv_indptr[:-1]).to(torch.int32)
   200→
   201→    info = get_mla_metadata_info_v1(
   202→        bs, qseqlen, nq, q_dtype, kv_dtype,
   203→        is_sparse=False, fast_mode=False,
   204→        num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits, intra_batch_mode=True,
   205→    )
   206→    work = [torch.empty(s, dtype=t, device="cuda") for s, t in info]
   207→    work_metadata, work_indptr, work_info_set, reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map = work
   208→
   209→    get_mla_metadata_v1(
   210→        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_last_page_len,
   211→        nq // nkv, nkv, True,
   212→        work_metadata, work_info_set, work_indptr,
   213→        reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map,
   214→        page_size=PAGE_SIZE, kv_granularity=max(PAGE_SIZE, 16),
   215→        max_seqlen_qo=qseqlen, uni_seqlen_qo=qseqlen,
   216→        fast_mode=False, max_split_per_batch=num_kv_splits,
   217→        intra_batch_mode=True, dtype_q=q_dtype, dtype_kv=kv_dtype,
   218→    )
   219→
   220→    total_kv_len = int(kv_indptr[-1].item())
   221→    kv_indices = torch.arange(total_kv_len, dtype=torch.int32, device="cuda")
   222→
   223→    # Pre-allocate intermediates
   224→    total_q = bs * qseqlen
   225→    logits   = torch.empty((num_kv_splits, total_q, nq, V_HEAD_DIM), dtype=torch.float32, device="cuda")
   226→    attn_lse = torch.empty((num_kv_splits, total_q, nq), dtype=torch.float32, device="cuda")
   227→    output   = torch.empty((total_q, nq, V_HEAD_DIM), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device="cuda")
   228→
   229→    meta = {
   230→        "work_meta_data":    work_metadata,
   231→        "work_indptr":       work_indptr,
   232→        "work_info_set":     work_info_set,
   233→        "reduce_indptr":     reduce_indptr,
   234→        "reduce_final_map":  reduce_final_map,
   235→        "reduce_partial_map": reduce_partial_map,
   236→        "kv_indices":        kv_indices,
   237→        "kv_last_page_len":  kv_last_page_len,
   238→        "logits":            logits,
   239→        "attn_lse":          attn_lse,
   240→        "output":            output,
   241→    }
   242→    _cache[key] = meta
   243→    return meta
   244→
   245→
   246→def _launch_mla_stage1(
   247→    q_fp8: torch.Tensor,
   248→    kv_4d: torch.Tensor,
   249→    qo_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   250→    kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   251→    meta: dict,
   252→    q_scale: torch.Tensor,
   253→    kv_scale: torch.Tensor,
   254→    qseqlen: int,
   255→    num_kv_splits: int,
   256→) -> None:
   257→    """Launch mla_a8w8_qh16_qseqlen1_gqaratio16_ps directly via hipModuleLaunchKernel.
   258→
   259→    Replaces the Python wrapper `mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd`, eliminating the
   260→    per-call dispatch overhead while keeping the same kernel execution path.
   261→    """
   262→    logits   = meta["logits"]
   263→    attn_lse = meta["attn_lse"]
   264→    output   = meta["output"]
   265→
   266→    # ── Scalar computations (matching csrc/py_itfs_cu/asm_mla.cu) ──
   267→    # s_Q_Bs = Q.stride(0) * element_size * max_seqlen_q
   268→    s_Q_Bs = q_fp8.stride(0) * q_fp8.element_size() * qseqlen
   269→    # s_Bs   = KV.stride(0) * element_size
   270→    s_Bs   = kv_4d.stride(0) * kv_4d.element_size()
   271→    # s_log2_plen = log2(PAGE_SIZE) = log2(1) = 0
   272→    s_log2_plen = 0
   273→    # s_MQA  = gqa_ratio * max_seqlen_q = (NUM_HEADS / NUM_KV_HEADS) * qseqlen
   274→    s_MQA  = (NUM_HEADS // NUM_KV_HEADS) * qseqlen
   275→
   276→    # ── Build the packed KernelArgs struct ──
   277→    args = MlaKernelArgs()
   278→    args.ptr_R          = logits.data_ptr()
   279→    args.ptr_LSE        = attn_lse.data_ptr()
   280→    args.ptr_Q          = q_fp8.data_ptr()
   281→    args.ptr_KV         = kv_4d.data_ptr()
   282→    args.ptr_LTP        = kv_indptr.data_ptr()
   283→    args.ptr_LTD        = meta["kv_indices"].data_ptr()
   284→    args.ptr_LTL        = meta["kv_last_page_len"].data_ptr()
   285→    args.scalar         = SM_SCALE
   286→    args.s_MQA          = s_MQA
   287→    args.s_kv_split     = num_kv_splits
   288→    args.s_Q_Bs         = s_Q_Bs
   289→    args.s_Bs           = s_Bs
   290→    args.s_log2_plen    = s_log2_plen
   291→    args.ptr_QTP        = qo_indptr.data_ptr()
   292→    # work_meta_data IS the pre-packed 80-byte uint64 GPU buffer — pass ptr directly
   293→    args.ptr_STP        = meta["work_meta_data"].data_ptr()
   294→    args.ptr_RP         = output.data_ptr()
   295→    args.ptr_QSCALE     = q_scale.data_ptr()
   296→    args.ptr_KVSCALE    = kv_scale.data_ptr()
   297→    args.out_16_nosplit = num_kv_splits
   298→    args.ptr_LSEP       = None  # no final LSE output needed
   299→
   300→    # ── Build config array for BUFFER_POINTER launch style ──
   301→    arg_size = ctypes.c_size_t(ctypes.sizeof(args))
   302→    config = (ctypes.c_void_p * 5)(
   303→        HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_BUFFER_POINTER,
   304→        ctypes.cast(ctypes.addressof(args), ctypes.c_void_p),
   305→        HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_BUFFER_SIZE,
   306→        ctypes.cast(ctypes.addressof(arg_size), ctypes.c_void_p),
   307→        HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_END,
   308→    )
   309→
   310→    # ── Persistent mode grid: (work_indptr.size(0) - 1, 1, 1) ──
   311→    gdx = meta["work_indptr"].size(0) - 1
   312→    stream = torch.cuda.current_stream().cuda_stream
   313→
   314→    ret = _hip.hipModuleLaunchKernel(
   315→        _mla_func,
   316→        gdx, 1, 1,   # grid
   317→        256, 1, 1,   # block (4 wavefronts of 64 threads)
   318→        0,           # shared memory
   319→        stream,
   320→        None,        # kernelParams — must be nullptr for BUFFER_POINTER style
   321→        config,      # extra
   322→    )
   323→    if ret != 0:
   324→        raise RuntimeError(f"hipModuleLaunchKernel failed with error code {ret}")
   325→
   326→
   327→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   328→# Entry point
   329→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   330→
   331→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
   332→    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config = data
   333→
   334→    bs       = config["batch_size"]
   335→    qseqlen  = config["q_seq_len"]
   336→    kvseqlen = config["kv_seq_len"]
   337→    total_kv = bs * kvseqlen
   338→
   339→    # ── Regime 1: torch.matmul for small shapes ──
   340→    # Small batch or short context: avoid aiter overhead entirely
   341→    if bs <= MATMUL_MAX_BS or total_kv <= MATMUL_MAX_TOTAL_KV:
   342→        kv_bf16 = kv_data["bf16"]  # [total_kv, 1, 576]
   343→        q_3d    = q.view(bs, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)            # [bs, 16, 576]
   344→        kv_per  = kv_bf16.view(bs, kvseqlen, QK_HEAD_DIM)       # [bs, kvseqlen, 576]
   345→        scores  = torch.matmul(q_3d, kv_per.transpose(1, 2))    # [bs, 16, kvseqlen]
   346→        scores.mul_(SM_SCALE)
   347→        weights = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(torch.bfloat16)
   348→        v_per   = kv_per[:, :, :V_HEAD_DIM]                     # [bs, kvseqlen, 512]
   349→        out     = torch.matmul(weights, v_per)                   # [bs, 16, 512]
   350→        return out.view(bs * qseqlen, NUM_HEADS, V_HEAD_DIM)
   351→
   352→    # ── Regime 2+3: direct CK-Tile ASM dispatch ──
   353→    kv_buffer_fp8, kv_scale = kv_data["fp8"]
   354→    q_fp8, q_scale = _quantize_fp8(q)
   355→
   356→    num_kv_splits = _choose_num_kv_splits(total_kv)
   357→    kv_4d = kv_buffer_fp8.view(kv_buffer_fp8.shape[0], PAGE_SIZE, NUM_KV_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)
   358→
   359→    meta = _get_cached_metadata(
   360→        bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen,
   361→        q_fp8.dtype, kv_buffer_fp8.dtype,
   362→        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, num_kv_splits,
   363→    )
   364→
   365→    # Stage 1: direct hipModuleLaunchKernel (bypasses Python wrapper overhead)
   366→    _launch_mla_stage1(
   367→        q_fp8.view(-1, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM),
   368→        kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr,
   369→        meta, q_scale, kv_scale,
   370→        qseqlen, num_kv_splits,
   371→    )
   372→
   373→    # Stage 2: reduce (must remain via aiter — kernel lives in .so, not .co)
   374→    mla_reduce_v1(
   375→        meta["logits"], meta["attn_lse"],
   376→        meta["reduce_indptr"], meta["reduce_final_map"], meta["reduce_partial_map"],
   377→        qseqlen, meta["output"], None,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    return meta["output"]
   381→

   127→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
   128→    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config = data
   129→
   130→    bs = config["batch_size"]
   131→    qseqlen = config["q_seq_len"]
   132→    kvseqlen = config["kv_seq_len"]
   133→    total_kv = bs * kvseqlen
   134→
   135→    # ── Regime 1: torch.matmul for small shapes ──
   136→    if bs <= MATMUL_MAX_BS or total_kv <= MATMUL_MAX_TOTAL_KV:
   137→        kv_bf16 = kv_data["bf16"]  # [total_kv, 1, 576]
   138→        # 3D batched matmul — no aiter overhead
   139→        q_3d = q.view(bs, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)  # [bs, 16, 576]
   140→        kv_per_batch = kv_bf16.view(bs, kvseqlen, QK_HEAD_DIM)  # [bs, kvseqlen, 576]
   141→        # Q @ K^T
   142→        scores = torch.matmul(q_3d, kv_per_batch.transpose(1, 2))  # [bs, 16, kvseqlen]
   143→        scores.mul_(SM_SCALE)
   144→        weights = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(torch.bfloat16)
   145→        # @ V (V = KV[:, :V_HEAD_DIM])
   146→        v_per_batch = kv_per_batch[:, :, :V_HEAD_DIM]  # [bs, kvseqlen, 512]
   147→        out = torch.matmul(weights, v_per_batch)  # [bs, 16, 512]
   148→        return out.view(bs * qseqlen, NUM_HEADS, V_HEAD_DIM)
   149→
   150→    # ── Regime 2+3: aiter direct ASM dispatch ──
   151→    kv_buffer_fp8, kv_scale = kv_data["fp8"]
   152→    q_fp8, q_scale = _quantize_fp8(q)
   153→
   154→    num_kv_splits = _choose_num_kv_splits(total_kv)
   155→    kv_4d = kv_buffer_fp8.view(kv_buffer_fp8.shape[0], PAGE_SIZE, NUM_KV_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)
   156→
   157→    meta = _get_cached_metadata(
   158→        bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen,
   159→        q_fp8.dtype, kv_buffer_fp8.dtype,
   160→        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, num_kv_splits,
   161→    )
   162→
   163→    output = meta["output"]
   164→    logits = meta["logits"]
   165→    attn_lse = meta["attn_lse"]
   166→
   167→    # Stage 1: ASM attention kernel
   168→    mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd(
   169→        q_fp8.view(-1, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM),

   171→        meta["kv_indices"], meta["kv_last_page_len"],

   177→    )

   183→        qseqlen, output, None,
   184→    )

   186→    return output

     1→"""
     2→Reference implementation for MLA (Multi-head Latent Attention) decode kernel.
     3→
     4→Uses aiter MLA kernels (mla_decode_fwd) as the reference.
     5→DeepSeek R1 forward_absorb MLA: absorbed q (576), compressed kv_buffer (576),
     6→output v_head_dim = kv_lora_rank = 512.
     7→
     8→The input provides:
     9→  q:       (total_q, 16, 576) bfloat16 — absorbed query
    10→  kv_data: dict with KV cache in three formats:
    11→    "bf16":  Tensor  (total_kv, 1, 576)  bfloat16          — highest precision
    12→    "fp8":   (Tensor, Tensor)  kv_buffer fp8 + scalar scale — per-tensor quantized
    13→    "mxfp4": (Tensor, Tensor)  kv_buffer fp4x2 + fp8_e8m0  — block-32 quantized
    14→  The reference quantizes Q to fp8 on-the-fly inside ref_kernel.
    15→
    16→The reference kernel quantizes Q to fp8 on-the-fly and uses fp8 KV (a8w8 kernel),
    17→which is ~2-3x faster than bf16 on MI355X with negligible accuracy loss.
    18→
    19→Decode only — persistent mode with get_mla_metadata_v1.
    20→"""
    21→
    22→import torch
    23→from aiter import dtypes as aiter_dtypes
    24→from aiter import get_mla_metadata_info_v1, get_mla_metadata_v1
    25→from aiter.mla import mla_decode_fwd
    26→from aiter.utility.fp4_utils import (
    27→    dynamic_mxfp4_quant,
    28→    e8m0_to_f32,
    29→    mxfp4_to_f32,
    30→)
    31→from task import input_t, output_t
    32→from utils import make_match_reference
    33→
    34→
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→# DeepSeek R1 latent MQA constants (forward_absorb path)
    37→# https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-0528/blob/main/config.json
    38→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    39→NUM_HEADS = 16
    40→NUM_KV_HEADS = 1
    41→KV_LORA_RANK = 512
    42→QK_ROPE_HEAD_DIM = 64
    43→QK_HEAD_DIM = KV_LORA_RANK + QK_ROPE_HEAD_DIM  # 576
    44→V_HEAD_DIM = KV_LORA_RANK  # 512
    45→SM_SCALE = 1.0 / (QK_HEAD_DIM**0.5)
    46→
    47→PAGE_SIZE = 1
    48→NUM_KV_SPLITS = 32
    49→
    50→# FP8 dtype (platform-specific via aiter)
    51→FP8_DTYPE = aiter_dtypes.fp8
    52→
    53→# Query dtype for the reference kernel: "fp8" or "bf16"
    54→Q_DTYPE = "fp8"
    55→
    56→# KV cache dtype for the reference kernel: "fp8" or "bf16"
    57→KV_DTYPE = "fp8"
    58→
    59→
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→# FP8 quantization (sglang style: dynamic per-tensor)
    62→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→
    65→def quantize_fp8(tensor: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    66→    """
    67→    Dynamic per-tensor FP8 quantization (following sglang scaled_fp8_quant).
    68→
    69→    Args:
    70→        tensor: bf16 tensor to quantize
    71→
    72→    Returns:
    73→        (fp8_tensor, scale) where scale is a scalar float32 tensor.
    74→        Dequantize: fp8_tensor.to(bf16) * scale
    75→    """
    76→    finfo = torch.finfo(FP8_DTYPE)
    77→    amax = tensor.abs().amax().clamp(min=1e-12)
    78→    scale = amax / finfo.max
    79→    fp8_tensor = (tensor / scale).clamp(min=finfo.min, max=finfo.max).to(FP8_DTYPE)
    80→    return fp8_tensor, scale.to(torch.float32).reshape(1)
    81→
    82→
    83→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    84→# MXFP4 quantization (aiter native: block-32, fp4x2 + fp8_e8m0 dtypes)
    85→# Uses aiter.utility.fp4_utils.dynamic_mxfp4_quant
    86→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    87→
    88→
    89→def quantize_mxfp4(tensor: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    90→    """
    91→    MXFP4 block-wise quantization using aiter's dynamic_mxfp4_quant.
    92→
    93→    Block size = 32. Each block gets an E8M0 scale factor.
    94→    Two FP4 E2M1 values are packed per byte.
    95→
    96→    Args:
    97→        tensor: bf16 tensor of shape [B, M, N] (N must be divisible by 32)
    98→
    99→    Returns:
   100→        (fp4_data, scale_e8m0)
   101→        - fp4_data:   shape [B, M, N//2] in aiter_dtypes.fp4x2
   102→        - scale_e8m0: shape [B*M, ceil(N/32)] padded, in aiter_dtypes.fp8_e8m0
   103→    """
   104→    orig_shape = tensor.shape  # (B, M, N)
   105→    B, M, N = orig_shape
   106→
   107→    # dynamic_mxfp4_quant expects 2D: (B*M, N)
   108→    tensor_2d = tensor.reshape(B * M, N)
   109→    fp4_data_2d, scale_e8m0 = dynamic_mxfp4_quant(tensor_2d)
   110→
   111→    # Reshape fp4_data back to 3D: (B, M, N//2)
   112→    fp4_data = fp4_data_2d.view(B, M, N // 2)
   113→
   114→    return fp4_data, scale_e8m0
   115→
   116→
   117→def dequantize_mxfp4(
   118→    fp4_data: torch.Tensor,
   119→    scale_e8m0: torch.Tensor,
   120→    orig_shape: tuple,
   121→    dtype: torch.dtype = torch.bfloat16,
   122→) -> torch.Tensor:
   123→    """
   124→    Dequantize MXFP4 tensor using aiter utilities.
   125→
   126→    Note: dynamic_mxfp4_quant may pad both row and block dimensions in scale_e8m0.
   127→    We trim scales to match the actual data dimensions.
   128→
   129→    Args:
   130→        fp4_data:   packed FP4 data, shape [B, M, N//2] in fp4x2 or uint8
   131→        scale_e8m0: E8M0 block scale factors (possibly padded) in fp8_e8m0
   132→        orig_shape: original (B, M, N) for reshaping
   133→        dtype:      output dtype
   134→
   135→    Returns:
   136→        Dequantized tensor of shape orig_shape.
   137→    """
   138→    B, M, N = orig_shape
   139→    num_rows = B * M
   140→    block_size = 32
   141→    num_blocks = N // block_size  # actual blocks needed (e.g. 576/32 = 18)
   142→
   143→    # Unpack FP4 to float32: mxfp4_to_f32 expects (..., N//2) -> (..., N)
   144→    fp4_data_2d = fp4_data.reshape(num_rows, N // 2)
   145→    float_vals = mxfp4_to_f32(fp4_data_2d)  # (num_rows, N)
   146→
   147→    # Convert E8M0 scales to float32 and trim padded dimensions
   148→    scale_f32 = e8m0_to_f32(scale_e8m0)  # (padded_rows, padded_blocks)
   149→    scale_f32 = scale_f32[:num_rows, :num_blocks]  # (num_rows, num_blocks)
   150→
   151→    # Apply block scales
   152→    float_vals_blocked = float_vals.view(num_rows, num_blocks, block_size)
   153→    scaled = float_vals_blocked * scale_f32.unsqueeze(-1)
   154→
   155→    return scaled.view(B, M, N).to(dtype)
   156→
   157→
   158→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   159→# Persistent mode metadata helpers
   160→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   161→
   162→
   163→def _make_mla_decode_metadata(
   164→    batch_size: int,
   165→    max_q_len: int,
   166→    nhead: int,
   167→    nhead_kv: int,
   168→    q_dtype: torch.dtype,
   169→    kv_dtype: torch.dtype,
   170→    qo_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   171→    kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   172→    kv_last_page_len: torch.Tensor,
   173→    num_kv_splits: int = NUM_KV_SPLITS,
   174→):
   175→    """Allocate and populate work buffers for persistent mla_decode_fwd."""
   176→    info = get_mla_metadata_info_v1(
   177→        batch_size,
   178→        max_q_len,
   179→        nhead,
   180→        q_dtype,
   181→        kv_dtype,
   182→        is_sparse=False,
   183→        fast_mode=False,
   184→        num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits,
   185→        intra_batch_mode=True,
   186→    )
   187→    work = [torch.empty(s, dtype=t, device="cuda") for s, t in info]
   188→    (
   189→        work_metadata,
   190→        work_indptr,
   191→        work_info_set,
   192→        reduce_indptr,
   193→        reduce_final_map,
   194→        reduce_partial_map,
   195→    ) = work
   196→
   197→    # Populate the metadata buffers
   198→    get_mla_metadata_v1(
   199→        qo_indptr,
   200→        kv_indptr,
   201→        kv_last_page_len,
   202→        nhead // nhead_kv,  # num_heads_per_head_k
   203→        nhead_kv,  # num_heads_k
   204→        True,  # is_causal
   205→        work_metadata,
   206→        work_info_set,
   207→        work_indptr,
   208→        reduce_indptr,
   209→        reduce_final_map,
   210→        reduce_partial_map,
   211→        page_size=PAGE_SIZE,
   212→        kv_granularity=max(PAGE_SIZE, 16),
   213→        max_seqlen_qo=max_q_len,
   214→        uni_seqlen_qo=max_q_len,
   215→        fast_mode=False,
   216→        max_split_per_batch=num_kv_splits,
   217→        intra_batch_mode=True,
   218→        dtype_q=q_dtype,
   219→        dtype_kv=kv_dtype,
   220→    )
   221→
   222→    return {
   223→        "work_meta_data": work_metadata,
   224→        "work_indptr": work_indptr,
   225→        "work_info_set": work_info_set,
   226→        "reduce_indptr": reduce_indptr,
   227→        "reduce_final_map": reduce_final_map,
   228→        "reduce_partial_map": reduce_partial_map,
   229→    }
   230→
   231→
   232→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   233→# Aiter reference kernel (decode only)
   234→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   235→
   236→
   237→def _aiter_mla_decode(
   238→    q: torch.Tensor,
   239→    kv_buffer: torch.Tensor,
   240→    qo_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   241→    kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,
   242→    config: dict,
   243→    q_scale: torch.Tensor | None = None,
   244→    kv_scale: torch.Tensor | None = None,
   245→) -> torch.Tensor:
   246→    """
   247→    MLA decode attention using aiter persistent-mode kernel.
   248→
   249→    Supports multiple Q/KV dtype combinations:
   250→      - Q_DTYPE="fp8":  fp8 Q + fp8 KV (a8w8) — fastest on MI355X
   251→      - Q_DTYPE="bf16": bf16 Q + bf16 KV (a16w16) — highest precision
   252→
   253→    q:          (total_q, num_heads, 576)  fp8 or bf16
   254→    kv_buffer:  (total_kv, 1, 576)         fp8 or bf16
   255→    q_scale:    scalar float32 (required for fp8 Q, None for bf16)
   256→    kv_scale:   scalar float32 (required for fp8 KV, None for bf16)
   257→    """
   258→    batch_size = config["batch_size"]
   259→    nq = config["num_heads"]
   260→    nkv = config["num_kv_heads"]
   261→    dq = config["qk_head_dim"]
   262→    dv = config["v_head_dim"]
   263→    q_seq_len = config["q_seq_len"]
   264→
   265→    total_kv_len = int(kv_indptr[-1].item())
   266→    kv_indices = torch.arange(total_kv_len, dtype=torch.int32, device="cuda")
   267→
   268→    # Reshape kv_buffer to 4D for aiter: (total_kv, page_size, nhead_kv, dim)
   269→    kv_buffer_4d = kv_buffer.view(kv_buffer.shape[0], PAGE_SIZE, nkv, kv_buffer.shape[-1])
   270→
   271→    max_q_len = q_seq_len
   272→    kv_last_page_len = (kv_indptr[1:] - kv_indptr[:-1]).to(torch.int32)
   273→
   274→    # Build persistent-mode metadata
   275→    meta = _make_mla_decode_metadata(
   276→        batch_size,
   277→        max_q_len,
   278→        nq,
   279→        nkv,
   280→        q.dtype,
   281→        kv_buffer.dtype,
   282→        qo_indptr,
   283→        kv_indptr,
   284→        kv_last_page_len,
   285→        num_kv_splits=NUM_KV_SPLITS,
   286→    )
   287→
   288→    o = torch.empty((q.shape[0], nq, dv), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device="cuda")
   289→    mla_decode_fwd(
   290→        q.view(-1, nq, dq),
   291→        kv_buffer_4d,
   292→        o,
   293→        qo_indptr,
   294→        kv_indptr,
   295→        kv_indices,
   296→        kv_last_page_len,
   297→        max_q_len,
   298→        page_size=PAGE_SIZE,
   299→        nhead_kv=nkv,
   300→        sm_scale=SM_SCALE,
   301→        logit_cap=0.0,
   302→        num_kv_splits=NUM_KV_SPLITS,
   303→        q_scale=q_scale,
   304→        kv_scale=kv_scale,
   305→        intra_batch_mode=True,
   306→        **meta,
   307→    )
   308→    return o
   309→
   310→
   311→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   312→# generate_input / ref_kernel / check_implementation
   313→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   314→
   315→
   316→def generate_input(batchsize: int, qseqlen: int, kvseqlen: int, seed: int) -> input_t:
   317→    """
   318→    Generate absorbed q and compressed kv_buffer for MLA decode.
   319→
   320→    Returns all three KV cache formats in kv_data dict:
   321→      kv_data = {
   322→        "bf16":  Tensor               — (total_kv, 1, 576) bfloat16
   323→        "fp8":   (Tensor, Tensor)     — kv_buffer fp8 + scalar scale
   324→        "mxfp4": (Tensor, Tensor)     — kv_buffer fp4x2 + fp8_e8m0 scale
   325→      }
   326→    """
   327→    gen = torch.Generator(device="cuda")
   328→    gen.manual_seed(seed)
   329→
   330→    total_q = batchsize * qseqlen
   331→    total_kv = batchsize * kvseqlen
   332→
   333→    # Absorbed query: (total_q, num_heads, 576) bf16
   334→    q = torch.randn(
   335→        (total_q, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM),
   336→        dtype=torch.bfloat16,
   337→        device="cuda",
   338→        generator=gen,
   339→    )
   340→
   341→    # Compressed KV buffer: (total_kv, 1, 576) bf16 — the source of truth
   342→    kv_buffer_bf16 = torch.randn(
   343→        (total_kv, NUM_KV_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM),
   344→        dtype=torch.bfloat16,
   345→        device="cuda",
   346→        generator=gen,
   347→    )
   348→
   349→    # Quantize KV to fp8
   350→    kv_buffer_fp8, kv_scale_fp8 = quantize_fp8(kv_buffer_bf16)
   351→
   352→    # Quantize KV to mxfp4
   353→    kv_buffer_mxfp4, kv_scale_mxfp4 = quantize_mxfp4(kv_buffer_bf16)
   354→
   355→    # All three KV formats: bf16 is a Tensor, fp8/mxfp4 are (Tensor, Tensor) tuples
   356→    kv_data = {
   357→        "bf16": kv_buffer_bf16,
   358→        "fp8": (kv_buffer_fp8, kv_scale_fp8),
   359→        "mxfp4": (kv_buffer_mxfp4, kv_scale_mxfp4),
   360→    }
   361→
   362→    qo_indptr = torch.arange(0, batchsize + 1, dtype=torch.int32, device="cuda") * qseqlen
   363→    kv_indptr = torch.arange(0, batchsize + 1, dtype=torch.int32, device="cuda") * kvseqlen
   364→
   365→    config = {
   366→        "batch_size": batchsize,
   367→        "num_heads": NUM_HEADS,
   368→        "num_kv_heads": NUM_KV_HEADS,
   369→        "qk_head_dim": QK_HEAD_DIM,
   370→        "kv_lora_rank": KV_LORA_RANK,
   371→        "qk_rope_head_dim": QK_ROPE_HEAD_DIM,
   372→        "v_head_dim": V_HEAD_DIM,
   373→        "q_seq_len": qseqlen,
   374→        "kv_seq_len": kvseqlen,
   375→        "sm_scale": SM_SCALE,
   376→    }
   377→
   378→    return (q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config)
   379→
   380→
   381→def ref_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
   382→    """Reference MLA decode attention. Uses Q_DTYPE and KV_DTYPE to select kernel variant."""
   383→    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config = data
   384→
   385→    # Resolve Q
   386→    if Q_DTYPE == "fp8":
   387→        q_input, q_scale = quantize_fp8(q)
   388→    else:
   389→        q_input, q_scale = q, None
   390→
   391→    # Resolve KV
   392→    if KV_DTYPE == "fp8":
   393→        kv_buffer_fp8, kv_scale = kv_data["fp8"]
   394→        kv_input = kv_buffer_fp8
   395→    else:
   396→        kv_input, kv_scale = kv_data["bf16"], None
   397→
   398→    return _aiter_mla_decode(
   399→        q_input,
   400→        kv_input,
   401→        qo_indptr,
   402→        kv_indptr,
   403→        config,
   404→        q_scale=q_scale,
   405→        kv_scale=kv_scale,
   406→    )
   407→
   408→
   409→check_implementation = make_match_reference(ref_kernel, rtol=1e-01, atol=1e-01)
   410→

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The harness provides a `hipStream_t` for timing — ALL GPU work must land on that stream. Our ctypes `hipModuleLaunchKernel` call uses `torch.cuda.current_stream().cuda_stream` but the harness uses a HIP-level stream that may not match PyTorch's stream handle. However, there IS a working path: **CUDA graphs** — PyTorch's official mechanism to replay captured GPU command sequences with ~zero Python overhead. CUDA graphs don't create new streams; they replay on the current stream, satisfying the harness constraint.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Let me now implement the CUDA graph approach. This eliminates ~40-50µs of Python dispatch overhead without any ctypes:

info]\n    work_metadata, work_indptr, work_info_set, reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map = work\n\n    get_mla_metadata_v1(\n        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_last_page_len,\n        nq // nkv, nkv, True,\n        work_metadata, work_info_set, work_indptr,\n        reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map,\n        page_size=PAGE_SIZE, kv_granularity=max(PAGE_SIZE, 16),\n        max_seqlen_qo=qseqlen, uni_seqlen_qo=qseqlen,\n        fast_mode=False, max_split_per_batch=num_kv_splits,\n        intra_batch_mode=True, dtype_q=q_dtype, dtype_kv=kv_dtype,\n    )\n\n    total_kv_len = int(kv_indptr[-1].item())\n    total_q = bs * qseqlen\n    meta = {\n        \"kv_indices\":         torch.arange(total_kv_len, dtype=torch.int32, device=\"cuda\"),\n        \"kv_last_page_len\":   kv_last_page_len,\n        \"work_meta_data\":     work_metadata,\n        \"work_indptr\":        work_indptr,\n        \"work_info_set\":      work_info_set,\n        \"reduce_indptr\":      reduce_indptr,\n        \"reduce_final_map\":   reduce_final_map,\n        \"reduce_partial_map\": reduce_partial_map,\n        \"logits\":   torch.empty((num_kv_splits, total_q, nq, V_HEAD_DIM), dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\"),\n        \"attn_lse\": torch.empty((num_kv_splits, total_q, nq), dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\"),\n        \"output\":   torch.empty((total_q, nq, V_HEAD_DIM), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\"),\n    }\n    _meta_cache[key] = meta\n    return meta\n\n\ndef _normal_dispatch(\n    q_fp8_3d: torch.Tensor,\n    kv_4d: torch.Tensor,\n    qo_indptr: torch.Tensor,\n    kv_indptr: torch.Tensor,\n    meta: dict,\n    q_scale: torch.Tensor,\n    kv_scale: torch.Tensor,\n    qseqlen: int,\n    num_kv_splits: int,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Python-dispatch path: two kernel calls, ~40-50µs Python overhead total.\"\"\"\n    mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd(\n        q_fp8_3d, kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr,\n        meta[\"kv_indices\"], meta[\"kv_last_page_len\"],\n        None,                                            # num_kv_splits_indptr\n        meta[\"work_meta_data\"], meta[\"work_indptr\"], meta[\"work_info_set\"],\n        qseqlen, PAGE_SIZE, NUM_KV_HEADS, SM_SCALE,\n        meta[\"logits\"], meta[\"attn_lse\"], meta[\"output\"],\n        q_scale, kv_scale,\n    )\n    mla_reduce_v1(\n        meta[\"logits\"], meta[\"attn_lse\"],\n        meta[\"reduce_indptr\"], meta[\"reduce_final_map\"], meta[\"reduce_partial_map\"],\n        qseqlen, meta[\"output\"], None,\n    )\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# Entry point\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\ndef custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:\n    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config = data\n    bs       = config[\"batch_size\"]\n    qseqlen  = config[\"q_seq_len\"]\n    kvseqlen = config[\"kv_seq_len\"]\n    total_kv = bs * kvseqlen\n\n    # ── Regime 1: torch.matmul for small shapes ──\n    if bs <= MATMUL_MAX_BS or total_kv <= MATMUL_MAX_TOTAL_KV:\n        kv_bf16 = kv_data[\"bf16\"]\n        q_3d    = q.view(bs, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)\n        kv_per  = kv_bf16.view(bs, kvseqlen, QK_HEAD_DIM)\n        scores  = torch.matmul(q_3d, kv_per.transpose(1, 2)).mul_(SM_SCALE)\n        weights = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(torch.bfloat16)\n        return torch.matmul(weights, kv_per[:, :, :V_HEAD_DIM]).view(bs * qseqlen, NUM_HEADS, V_HEAD_DIM)\n\n    # ── Regime 2+3: ASM kernel via CUDA graph (or fallback) ──\n    kv_buffer_fp8, kv_scale = kv_data[\"fp8\"]\n    q_fp8, q_scale = _quantize_fp8(q)\n    num_kv_splits = _choose_num_kv_splits(total_kv)\n    kv_4d = kv_buffer_fp8.view(kv_buffer_fp8.shape[0], PAGE_SIZE, NUM_KV_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)\n    meta = _get_meta(\n        bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen,\n        q_fp8.dtype, kv_buffer_fp8.dtype,\n        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, num_kv_splits,\n    )\n    q_fp8_3d  = q_fp8.view(-1, NUM_HEADS, QK_HEAD_DIM)\n    shape_key = (bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen, num_kv_splits)\n\n    gi = _graph_cache.get(shape_key)\n\n    # ── Fast path: graph already captured ──\n    if gi is not None and gi is not False:\n        if (kv_4d.data_ptr()      == gi[\"kv_ptr\"]\n                and qo_indptr.data_ptr() == gi[\"qo_ptr\"]\n                and kv_indptr.data_ptr() == gi[\"ki_ptr\"]):\n            # Address-stable: copy only q_fp8 (~288KB ≈ 0.5µs), then replay\n            gi[\"sq\"].copy_(q_fp8_3d)\n            gi[\"sqs\"].copy_(q_scale)\n            gi[\"sks\"].copy_(kv_scale)\n            gi[\"graph\"].replay()\n            return meta[\"output\"]\n        # Addresses changed (test mode diff allocs) → fall through to normal\n        _normal_dispatch(q_fp8_3d, kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, meta, q_scale, kv_scale, qseqlen, num_kv_splits)\n        return meta[\"output\"]\n\n    # ── Graph already attempted but failed ──\n    if gi is False:\n        _normal_dispatch(q_fp8_3d, kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, meta, q_scale, kv_scale, qseqlen, num_kv_splits)\n        return meta[\"output\"]\n\n    # ── Warmup call (first time for this shape) ──\n    if shape_key not in _warmup_done:\n        _warmup_done.add(shape_key)\n        _normal_dispatch(q_fp8_3d, kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, meta, q_scale, kv_scale, qseqlen, num_kv_splits)\n        return meta[\"output\"]\n\n    # ── Second call: capture CUDA graph ──\n    # Pre-allocate static q tensors — content updated before every replay\n    static_q  = q_fp8_3d.clone()    # same shape, fp8, fixed address\n    static_qs = q_scale.clone()     # (1,) float32\n    static_ks = kv_scale.clone()    # (1,) float32\n\n    # Copy current values so graph capture AND first replay use correct data\n    static_q.copy_(q_fp8_3d)\n    static_qs.copy_(q_scale)\n    static_ks.copy_(kv_scale)\n\n    try:\n        g = torch.cuda.CUDAGraph()\n        with torch.cuda.graph(g):\n            # kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr are captured at their current addresses.\n            # Benchmark runner reuses the same tensor objects every call →\n            # replay reads fresh content from stable addresses.\n            _normal_dispatch(\n                static_q, kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr,\n                meta, static_qs, static_ks,\n                qseqlen, num_kv_splits,\n            )\n\n        # Execute the captured graph to produce this call's result\n        g.replay()\n\n        _graph_cache[shape_key] = {\n            \"graph\": g,\n            \"sq\":    static_q,\n            \"sqs\":   static_qs,\n            \"sks\":   static_ks,\n            \"kv_ptr\": kv_4d.data_ptr(),\n            \"qo_ptr\": qo_indptr.data_ptr(),\n            \"ki_ptr\": kv_indptr.data_ptr(),\n        }\n\n    except Exception:\n        # Graph capture failed (e.g. aiter has CPU-GPU sync) → always use normal\n        _graph_cache[shape_key] = False\n        _normal_dispatch(q_fp8_3d, kv_4d, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, meta, q_scale, kv_scale, qseqlen, num_kv_splits)\n\n    return meta[\"output\"]\n"

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
CUDA graphs work by recording a GPU command sequence once and replaying it. The key subtlety: graphs don't capture data — they capture *addresses*. So copying fresh q_fp8 into `static_q` (fixed address) before each replay ensures the kernel reads current values. The kv_4d tensor relies on the benchmark runner reusing the same pre-allocated buffer (address stable, content changes) — the graph automatically reads the updated content.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Now run the correctness test:

> AGENT

The same "work on another stream" error fires for CUDA graphs too. `torch.cuda.graph()` internally creates a **side capture stream** — PyTorch does this to avoid contaminating the timing stream with graph capture overhead. The harness detects this side stream as a violation. Both ctypes dispatch AND CUDA graph capture are blocked.

Let me isolate the issue by testing a "no-graph" control version:

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Luma AMD Speedrun — Direct CK Dispatch Breakthrough

**Status**: PENDING
**Session**: 3 (continuation from sessions 1-2)
**Last updated**: 2026-03-24
**Knowledge graph**: SurrealDB `luma_speedrun` namespace + `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/luma-speedrun/`

---

## Executive Summary

Three GPU kernels for AMD MI355X (gfx950) competition. The **API ceiling** (~20-25µs Python
dispatch per torch op) prevents matching leaders who use single fused kernels.

| Kernel | Our Score | Leader | Gap | Status |
|--------|-----------|--------|-----|--------|
| MLA | ~70µs | ~33µs | 2.1x | ⚠ Plateau |
| MoE | ~180µs | ~110µs | 1.6x | ⚠ Plateau |
| GEMM | ~13.4µs | ~4.3µs | 3.1x | ⚠ Plateau |

**Active breakthrough path**: Direct `hipModuleLaunchKernel` via ctypes bypassing all Python
wrapper layers. APIs are confirmed available. The only blocker: kernel argument layout.

---

## Knowledge Graph Schema

### Design Principle
Experiments form a **directed graph of evidence** — not a linear task list. Each node stores
findings that may unblock multiple future paths. Fresh agents query this graph to find
unexplored approaches without reading full session history.

### SurrealDB Schema (namespace: `luma_speedrun`)

```surql
-- Nodes (UPSERT by id to avoid duplicates)
DEFINE TABLE experiment SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD id         ON experiment TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD kernel     ON experiment TYPE string;   -- mla|moe|gemm
DEFINE FIELD approach   ON experiment TYPE string;   -- strategy name
DEFINE FIELD session    ON experiment TYPE int;      -- session number
DEFINE FIELD status     ON experiment TYPE string;   -- confirmed|failed|blocked|pending
DEFINE FIELD score_us   ON experiment TYPE option<float>;  -- timing in µs (null if failed)
DEFINE FIELD finding    ON experiment TYPE string;   -- key finding in 1-2 sentences
DEFINE FIELD evidence   ON experiment TYPE string;   -- raw output/numbers
DEFINE FIELD created    ON experiment TYPE datetime;

DEFINE TABLE blocker SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD id          ON blocker TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD description ON blocker TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD resolved    ON blocker TYPE bool DEFAULT false;
DEFINE FIELD resolution  ON blocker TYPE option<string>;

-- Edges (bidirectional via symmetric RELATE)
DEFINE TABLE failed_because  SCHEMALESS;  -- experiment -> blocker
DEFINE TABLE resolved_by     SCHEMALESS;  -- blocker -> experiment
DEFINE TABLE enables         SCHEMALESS;  -- experiment -> experiment (A finding enables B)
DEFINE TABLE contradicts     SCHEMALESS;  -- experiment -> experiment (A disproves B)
DEFINE TABLE based_on        SCHEMALESS;  -- experiment -> experiment (B builds on A)
```

### Obsidian Vault Structure
```
~/vaults/cohezion-vault/luma-speedrun/
  README.md                    ← entry point, links to all experiments
  experiments/
    MLA-001-warmup-removal.md  ← each experiment as a note
    MLA-002-sdpa-fusion.md
    MLA-003-ctypes-probe-v1.md
    MLA-004-ctypes-probe-v2.md
    MOE-001-warmup-removal.md
    MOE-002-expert-mask.md
    GEMM-001-tritonblas.md
  blockers/
    B001-kernel-arg-layout.md  ← active blocker with [[wikilinks]]
    B002-sdpa-kv-dim-mismatch.md
  discoveries/
    D001-hip-apis-available.md
    D002-name-mangling-pattern.md
    D003-co-kernel-inventory.md
  approaches/
    A001-direct-ck-dispatch.md ← the breakthrough approach
```

## Session History (Full Traceability)

### Session 1 — Infrastructure + Initial Submissions

**Completed work**:
- Built `autoresearch/popcorn.py` + `autoresearch/driver.py` (K-Search tree)
- Submitted all 3 kernels to leaderboard (first ranked scores)

### Session 2 — Probe Campaign

#### MLA-001: Warmup Removal (MoE)
- **File**: `luma_speedrun/amd-moe-mxfp4/submission.py`
- **Hypothesis**: 8-expert dummy warmup poisoning CK autotuning cache for 256-expert shapes
- **Result**: ❌ No improvement (~180µs unchanged)
- **Finding**: CK uses shape-specific cache keys; 8-expert shapes never collide with 256-expert
- **SurrealDB node**: `MOE-001` status=failed
- **Disproves**: "Warmup cache collision is causing suboptimal CK selection"

#### MOE-002: Expert Mask (Sparse Dispatch)
- **File**: `luma_speedrun/amd-moe-mxfp4/submission_expert_mask.py`
- **Hypothesis**: `expert_mask` skips ~200 empty experts in sorting/dispatch
- **Result**: ❌ 30,624 mismatches on 257-expert shapes (wrong output)
- **Finding**: `fused_moe(expert_mask=...)` produces corrupt output for 256-expert padding shapes
- **SurrealDB node**: `MOE-002` status=failed
- **Do not retry**: This approach is confirmed broken for the competition shapes

#### MLA-002: SDPA Fusion
- **File**: `luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/submission_sdpa.py`
- **Hypothesis**: Single SDPA call fuses 4 torch ops (Q@K^T, scale, softmax, @V)
- **Result**: ❌ MASSIVE REGRESSION — 1542µs (vs ~70µs baseline) for bs=32, kvseqlen=1024
- **Finding**: PyTorch SDPA on MI355X gfx950 falls back to slow general path when K_head_dim ≠ V_head_dim (576 vs 512)
- **SurrealDB node**: `MLA-002` status=failed, score_us=1542
- **Do not retry**: MLA's K≠V dimension is an architectural constraint, not configurable

#### MLA-003: ctypes Probe v1
- **File**: `luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/probes/probe_direct_ck.py`
- **Result**: ✅ PARTIAL — libamdhip64.so loads, hipModuleLoad works, 1372 .co files found
- **Blocked**: hipModuleGetFunction failed (hipErrorNotFound=500) — wrong kernel names guessed
- **Finding**: Runner does NOT block ctypes; filesystem access to .co files confirmed
- **SurrealDB node**: `MLA-003` status=blocked, blocker=`B002-wrong-kernel-names`

#### MLA-004: ctypes Probe v2 (BREAKTHROUGH PARTIAL)
- **File**: `luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/probes/probe_direct_ck_v2.py`
- **Result**: ✅ CONFIRMED — 6 MLA kernel function handles obtained
- **Key discovery**: C++ name mangling: `_ZN5aiter{len(name)}{name}E`
- **Key discovery**: `hipModuleLaunchKernel` confirmed available (function pointer obtained)
- **Key discovery**: `tensor.data_ptr()` works for GPU memory pointers
- **Still blocked**: Kernel argument layout unknown — cannot launch without correct struct
- **SurrealDB node**: `MLA-004` status=blocked, blocker=`B001-kernel-arg-layout`

#### MLA-005 / MOE-003: Source Reading Probes
- **Files**: `probe_asm_source.py`, `probe_asm_source_v2.py`
- **Result**: ❌ BLOCKED — runner returns "work on another stream" error for filesystem glob
- **Finding**: Runner sandboxes filesystem reads of aiter Python source files
- **Workaround**: Must read aiter source **offline** (clone repo locally)
- **SurrealDB node**: `MLA-005` status=failed, blocker=`B003-runner-sandbox`

#### GEMM-001: tritonblas + gemm_a4w4_asm k_split sweep
- **File**: `luma_speedrun/amd-mxfp4-mm/submission_tritonblas.py`
- **Result**: ~22µs — same as existing aiter path, no improvement
- **Finding**: The bottleneck is quantization (~26-84µs), not GEMM compute (~7-10µs)
- **SurrealDB node**: `GEMM-001` status=confirmed, score_us=22

## Active Blocker: B001 — Kernel Argument Layout Unknown

**Description**: `hipModuleLaunchKernel` requires a `void**` array of kernel argument pointers
in the exact order the C++ kernel expects. Wrong order → GPU fault or silent corruption.

**What we know**:
- The Python call to `mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd(...)` takes 18 arguments
- These get translated by aiter's C++ pybind11 layer to raw GPU pointers + scalars
- The argument struct layout is in aiter's C++ source (not accessible on runner)

**How to resolve**: Clone `https://github.com/ROCm/aiter` locally and read:
1. `op_tests/test_mla_decode.py` — shows how it's called
2. `aiter/ops/mla.py` or similar Python wrapper — shows argument unpacking
3. `csrc/py_itfs_ck.cpp` or `csrc/ck_mla_dispatch.cpp` — shows raw kernel call

**Resolution enables**: MLA-006 (direct dispatch submission)

## Phase B: Kernel Argument Discovery ✅ COMPLETE

**Agents**: kernel-researcher (session 3)
**Source**: `~/dev/aiter/csrc/py_itfs_cu/asm_mla.cu`
**Spec file**: `luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/probes/kernel_arg_spec.md`
**Vault**: `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/luma-speedrun/discoveries/D004-mla-stage1-arg-layout.md`

### Key Findings (B001 RESOLVED)

- **Struct**: 320 bytes, `_pack_ = 1`, 16-byte aligned fields via p2/p3 padding
- **Launch style**: `HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_BUFFER_POINTER` (extra args, NOT kernelParams[])
  - BUFFER_POINTER=0x01, BUFFER_SIZE=0x02, END=0x03
- **Grid (persistent)**: `(work_indptr.size(0) - 1, 1, 1)`
- **Block**: `(256, 1, 1)` — 4 wavefronts of 64 threads
- **Shared mem**: 0
- **ptr_STP**: use `work_meta_data.data_ptr()` directly (already pre-packed uint64[10] GPU buffer)
- **mla_reduce_v1**: NOT a .co kernel — compiled into libaiter.so, must call via Python

### Struct Field Order (byte offsets)
```
0:   ptr_R        → logits          (splitData f32)
16:  ptr_LSE      → attn_lse        (splitLse f32)
32:  ptr_Q        → q_fp8
48:  ptr_KV       → kv_4d
64:  ptr_LTP      → kv_indptr
80:  ptr_LTD      → kv_indices
96:  ptr_LTL      → kv_last_page_len
112: scalar        → SM_SCALE (float)
128: s_MQA        = NUM_HEADS * qseqlen = 16 (uint32)
144: s_kv_split   = num_kv_splits (uint32)
160: s_Q_Bs       = Q.stride(0)*elemsize*qseqlen = 9216 (uint32)
176: s_Bs         = KV.stride(0)*elemsize = 576 (uint32)
192: s_log2_plen  = log2(PAGE_SIZE) = 0 (uint32)
208: ptr_QTP      → qo_indptr
224: ptr_STP      → work_meta_data.data_ptr()
240: ptr_RP       → output (bf16)
256: ptr_QSCALE   → q_scale
272: ptr_KVSCALE  → kv_scale
288: out_16_nosplit = num_kv_splits (uint32, same as s_kv_split)
304: ptr_LSEP     → None (nullable)
```

## Phase C: Direct Dispatch Implementation (Session 3, Main Work)

**Agents**: Dispatch Engineer Agent
**Prerequisite**: Phase B complete (arg layout known)

### Task C.1: MLA Stage1 ctypes Launcher

**File**: `luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/submission_direct_ck.py` (NEW)

```python
"""Direct CK-Tile dispatch: bypass all Python wrapper overhead.

Strategy: hipModuleLaunchKernel with pre-compiled .co kernel objects.
Expected: eliminate 2-3 Python dispatch layers (~40-60µs overhead).
Target: approach ~33µs leader benchmark.
"""
import ctypes
import torch
from aiter import dtypes as aiter_dtypes

hip = ctypes.CDLL("libamdhip64.so")

# Pre-load kernel module at import time (not in hot path)
_CO_PATH = b"/home/runner/aiter/hsa/gfx950/mla/mla_a8w8_qh16_qseqlen1_gqaratio16_ps.co"
_KERNEL_NAME = b"_ZN5aiter36mla_a8w8_qh16_qseqlen1_gqaratio16_psE"

_module = ctypes.c_void_p()
hip.hipModuleLoad(ctypes.byref(_module), _CO_PATH)
_func = ctypes.c_void_p()
hip.hipModuleGetFunction(ctypes.byref(_func), _module, _KERNEL_NAME)

def _launch_mla_stage1(q, kv, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, ..., grid, block, smem):
    """Direct kernel launch — zero Python wrapper overhead."""
    # Build argument pointer array (layout from aiter C++ source)
    args = (ctypes.c_void_p * N_ARGS)(
        q.data_ptr(), kv.data_ptr(), qo_indptr.data_ptr(), ...
    )
    stream = torch.cuda.current_stream().cuda_stream
    hip.hipModuleLaunchKernel(
        _func,
        grid[0], grid[1], grid[2],
        block[0], block[1], block[2],
        smem, ctypes.c_void_p(stream),
        ctypes.cast(ctypes.byref(args), ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p)),
        None
    )
```

**Correctness check**: Run `popcorn-cli submit --mode test` FIRST before benchmark.
MLA tolerance is loose (rtol=0.1, atol=0.1) — more room for approximation errors.

### Task C.2: Fuse Stage1 + Reduce (if C.1 works)

Current aiter path: stage1 ASM + reduce_v1 = 2 Python dispatch calls.
If C.1 works, also probe direct dispatch of `mla_reduce_v1`:

```bash
ls /home/runner/aiter/hsa/gfx950/reduce/  # Check reduce kernel names
```

Fused path: launch stage1 → launch reduce → zero Python in between.
Expected additional gain: ~20-25µs (eliminate one more dispatch layer).

## Agent Team Structure

### Specialist Agents (Parallel Dispatch)

| Agent | Role | Phase |
|-------|------|-------|
| **Knowledge Curator** | Populates SurrealDB + vault from history | A (first) |
| **Kernel Research** | Clones aiter, reads C++ source offline | B (parallel) |
| **Dispatch Engineer** | Implements ctypes launcher | C (after B) |
| **MoE Specialist** | Focuses on fmoe_2stages kernel selection | C.3 (parallel) |
| **Verification** | Runs popcorn-cli test + benchmark cycles | C.1, C.2, C.3 |

### Cross-Agent Knowledge Sharing
All agents write findings to SurrealDB before exiting. The graph edges enable the
next agent to answer: "What has been tried? What does this finding enable?"

```
Knowledge Curator → creates graph → Kernel Research can query findings
Kernel Research → discovers arg layout → Dispatch Engineer finds it in graph
Dispatch Engineer → tests → writes result → any future agent sees score
```

---

## Execution Order

| Priority | Task | Prerequisite | Expected Gain |
|----------|------|--------------|---------------|
| 1 | **A.1-A.2**: Knowledge bootstrap | None (parallel with B) | Enables fresh agents |
| 2 | **B.1-B.3**: Clone aiter + read arg layout | None | Unblocks C |
| 3 | **C.1**: MLA direct dispatch probe | B complete | 40-60µs reduction → ~30µs |
| 4 | **C.2**: Fuse stage1 + reduce | C.1 correct | +20µs |
| 5 | **C.3**: MoE direct dispatch | C.1 validates approach | ~70µs reduction |
| 6 | **C.4**: GEMM direct dispatch | C.1 validates approach | Unknown |

**Critical path**: B.1 → B.2 → B.3 → C.1 → leaderboard

---

## Key Files

| File | State | Purpose |
|------|-------|---------|
| `luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/submission.py` | CURRENT BEST (~70µs) | Active submission |
| `luma_speedrun/amd-moe-mxfp4/submission.py` | CURRENT BEST (~180µs) | Active submission |
| `luma_speedrun/amd-mxfp4-mm/submission.py` | CURRENT BEST (~13.4µs) | Active submission |
| `luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/probes/probe_direct_ck_v2.py` | KEY PROBE | Confirmed 6 kernel handles |
| `~/.claude/skills/amd-ctypes-hip-kernel-dispatch/SKILL.md` | REFERENCE | Full HIP dispatch guide |
| `~/dev/aiter/` | TO CREATE | aiter source (clone task B.1) |
| `luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/submission_direct_ck.py` | TO CREATE | Direct dispatch (task C.1) |
| `luma_speedrun/amd-moe-mxfp4/submission_direct_ck.py` | TO CREATE | Direct dispatch (task C.3) |

---

## Verification Protocol

For every submission (in order):

1. `popcorn-cli submit --mode test` — correctness gate. **Do not benchmark until this passes.**
2. Compare timing to baseline: `popcorn-cli submit --mode benchmark`
3. Only `--mode leaderboard` if timing improvement > 5% over current best

**MLA tolerance**: rtol=0.1, atol=0.1 (loose — FP8 approximation expected)
**MoE tolerance**: Stricter — verify zero mismatches before benchmark
**GEMM tolerance**: Exact match required (quantization is deterministic)

---

## Known Dead Ends (Do Not Retry)

| Approach | Reason | Session |
|----------|--------|---------|
| `expert_mask` in fused_moe | 30,624 mismatches for 256-expert shapes | 2 |
| SDPA fusion for MLA | K≠V dims (576 vs 512) forces 1542µs slow path | 2 |
| Warmup removal (MoE) | CK cache is shape-keyed; no cache collision | 2 |
| Filesystem glob on runner | Runner sandbox blocks aiter source reads | 2 |
| tritonblas.matmul_fp4 | Same timing as aiter (~22µs); quant is the bottleneck | 2 |
| gemm_a4w4_asm k_split sweep | No improvement over default | 2 |

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The vault search returned empty — the `luma-speedrun` folder either doesn't exist yet or the knowledge curator from Phase A used a different path. We'll write all notes fresh. The key architectural insight to document: the runner's stream detection is a **profiling-level monitor**, not just a syscall filter — it catches GPU work appearing on unauthorized stream timelines even when the code looks legitimate from Python's perspective.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

{
  "learning_id": "MLA-006-ctypes-hip-stream-blocked",
  "title": "MLA-006: ctypes hipModuleLaunchKernel blocked by runner stream detection",
  "content": "Session 3, AMD MI355X (gfx950) Luma Speedrun.\n\nAPPROACH: Load mla_a8w8_qh16_qseqlen1_gqaratio16_ps.co via ctypes.CDLL(\"libamdhip64.so\"), obtain function handle via hipModuleGetFunction (correct C++ mangled name _ZN5aiter36mla_a8w8_qh16_qseqlen1_gqaratio16_psE), build 320-byte MlaKernelArgs struct with HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_BUFFER_POINTER, launch via hipModuleLaunchKernel using torch.cuda.current_stream().cuda_stream.\n\nSTATUS: BLOCKED. Test result: \"work on another stream\" error (runner returns 500 Internal Server Error).\n\nROOT CAUSE: The benchmarking harness records start/end timing events on a specific hipStream_t it creates internally. torch.cuda.current_stream().cuda_stream does NOT return this harness stream — it returns PyTorch's own default stream. When hipModuleLaunchKernel launches on this mismatched stream, the runner's profiling-level stream monitor detects the violation and aborts.\n\nWHAT WAS CONFIRMED WORKING: libamdhip64.so loads, hipModuleLoad succeeds, hipModuleGetFunction succeeds (6 kernel handles obtained in probe v2), HIP_LAUNCH_PARAM_BUFFER_POINTER launch API is correct, 320-byte MlaKernelArgs struct layout is correct per csrc analysis.\n\nWHY THIS IS A DEAD END: The harness stream handle is never exposed to user code — it is not in the `config` dict (verified by reading reference_implementation.py and task.py). There is no documented API to obtain the harness hipStream_t from within custom_kernel(). Any ctypes kernel launch will use the wrong stream.\n\nFAILED WORKAROUNDS: torch.cuda.current_stream() — wrong stream. Creating new stream — also wrong stream.\n\nENABLES: Understanding that ONLY standard PyTorch/aiter function calls correctly target the harness stream. Direct GPU kernel dispatch through any non-PyTorch pathway is blocked.\n\nFILE: luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/submission_direct_ck.py",
  "pattern": "amd-stream-detection",
  "score": 0.95
}

{
  "learning_id": "MLA-007-cuda-graph-side-stream-blocked",
  "title": "MLA-007: torch.cuda.CUDAGraph blocked — graph capture creates internal side stream",
  "content": "Session 3, AMD MI355X (gfx950) Luma Speedrun.\n\nAPPROACH: Use torch.cuda.CUDAGraph to capture mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd + mla_reduce_v1 kernel calls, then replay with only a q_fp8 copy (~0.5µs) before each replay. Expected to eliminate Python dispatch overhead (~40-50µs) while remaining within PyTorch's stream management.\n\nARCHITECTURE: \n- Call 1 per shape → warmup (normal _normal_dispatch)\n- Call 2 per shape → capture graph (torch.cuda.graph(g)) + replay\n- Call 3+ → static_q.copy_(q_fp8_3d) + g.replay() fast path\n- Address stability: kv_4d/qo_indptr/kv_indptr reuse same tensor objects → stable addresses captured in graph\n\nSTATUS: BLOCKED. Test result: SAME \"work on another stream\" error as MLA-006.\n\nROOT CAUSE: torch.cuda.graph() creates an INTERNAL SIDE STREAM for graph capture, separate from both PyTorch's default stream and the harness timing stream. Even though the captured operations (mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd, mla_reduce_v1) would individually use the correct stream during normal dispatch, the capture process routes through a private capture stream. The harness's stream monitor detects GPU work appearing on this unauthorized side stream timeline.\n\nSAME VIOLATION, DIFFERENT MECHANISM: MLA-006 launched on the wrong stream directly. MLA-007 captures on the wrong stream — the capture itself triggers the detector, not the replay.\n\nWHY THIS MATTERS: This disproves the hypothesis that \"CUDA graphs will work because they use PyTorch dispatch internally.\" PyTorch's CUDA graph implementation creates its own stream management layer that bypasses the harness stream at capture time.\n\nTHE UNIFYING PRINCIPLE: Any mechanism that creates GPU work on a stream other than the harness's specific hipStream_t is blocked. This includes: (1) ctypes direct kernel launch, (2) CUDA graph capture, (3) any self-created HIP stream. Only standard synchronous PyTorch/aiter function calls correctly land on the harness stream.\n\nFILE: luma_speedrun/amd-mixed-mla/submission_cudagraph.py\n\nENABLES: Understanding that the optimization ceiling for MLA must be pursued WITHIN standard PyTorch/aiter dispatch — kernel fusion, algorithm selection, or different quantization paths. Not by bypassing dispatch.",
  "pattern": "amd-stream-detection",
  "score": 0.95
}

{
  "learning_id": "D005-harness-stream-detection-mechanism",
  "title": "D005: Runner stream detection mechanism — all non-PyTorch GPU dispatch paths blocked",
  "content": "Session 3 discovery. AMD MI355X (gfx950) Luma Speedrun.\n\nDISCOVERY: The Popcorn CLI benchmark harness uses a profiling-level HIP stream monitor that detects GPU work appearing on any stream other than the harness's internal timing stream. This blocks ALL approaches that bypass PyTorch's standard kernel dispatch:\n\n1. ctypes hipModuleLaunchKernel → blocked (wrong stream)\n2. torch.cuda.CUDAGraph capture → blocked (internal side stream)\n3. Any self-created hipStream_t → would be blocked\n4. hipStreamCreate + launch → would be blocked\n\nWHY STANDARD DISPATCH WORKS: aiter's pybind11 functions (mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd, mla_reduce_v1) ultimately call into PyTorch's C++ kernel launch machinery, which is aware of the current \"active\" HIP stream set by the harness before calling custom_kernel(). This is why the baseline ~70µs solution works correctly.\n\nTHE ERROR SIGNAL: \"work on another stream\" / 500 Internal Server Error. This is the runner's generic violation message for BOTH stream violations AND filesystem sandbox violations. Same error message, different triggers.\n\nSTREAM HANDLE INACCESSIBILITY: The harness hipStream_t is NOT exposed in the `config` dict. The data tuple is exactly (q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config) — 5 elements. Config has: batch_size, num_heads, num_kv_heads, qk_head_dim, kv_lora_rank, qk_rope_head_dim, v_head_dim, q_seq_len, kv_seq_len, sm_scale. No stream.\n\nIMPLICATION FOR FUTURE SESSIONS: The 2.1x gap to leader (~70µs vs ~33µs) CANNOT be closed by bypassing Python dispatch. The gap must be closed by:\n(a) Better algorithm selection within aiter (fast_mode, kernel variant, num_kv_splits)\n(b) Reducing Python overhead within the aiter call sequence (fewer ops, better caching)\n(c) Different quantization approach (skip FP8 quant for some shapes)\n(d) Finding a fused kernel path within the aiter Python API\n\nRELATED DEAD ENDS: MLA-006 (ctypes), MLA-007 (CUDA graphs), MLA-005 (filesystem glob/sandbox)",
  "pattern": "amd-runner-constraints",
  "score": 0.98
}
